\displaystyle=\left\lVert\chi_{R}\cdot\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}

for all
R>0
.

Proof of (
6.72
).

First observe that for each
t\in[-\tau,\tau]
, we have

\mathcal{L}(t,-\tau;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}})){\xi}_{n}(-\tau+s_{n}^{s_{0}})\rightharpoonup\mathcal{L}(t,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)

weakly in
H^{1}
as
n\to\infty
. Furthermore,

\displaystyle\mathcal{L}(t,-\tau;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)}(-\tau+s_{n}^{s_{0}})

\displaystyle{}=\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}

\displaystyle{}\to\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}))\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}

in
L^{\infty}([-\tau,\tau],H^{1})
as
n\to\infty
. Hence, for each
R>0
and
t\in[-\tau,\tau]
,

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\chi_{R}\cdot\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))({\xi}_{n}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)})(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{4}_{x}}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\chi_{R}\cdot\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{4}_{x}}.

Furthermore,

\sup_{n}\left\lVert\chi_{R}(\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))({\xi}_{n}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)})(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}))\right\rVert_{L^{4}_{x}}\lesssim\sup_{n}\left\lVert\xi_{n}\right\rVert_{L^{\infty}H^{1}}.

Since a constant function belongs to
L^{8}_{t}([-\tau,\tau])
, (
6.72
) therefore follows from Lebesgue’s convergence theorem.
∎

Next, observe that as
y^{(s_{0},k)}=\infty
for
k\geqslant 1
, we have

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\chi_{R}\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}=0

(6.73)

for any
k\geqslant 0
and
\tau>0
.

Inserting (
6.12
), (
6.72
), and (
6.73
) into (
6.71
), we see that

\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}=0.

(6.74)

Using this, we will prove below that
{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}
scatters to
\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}
backward in time, i.e.

\lim_{t\to-\infty}\left\lVert{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(t)-\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{H^{1}}=0.

(6.75)

On the other hand, we have

\displaystyle\lim_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}};z_{n})(\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)})(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}

\displaystyle=\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}))\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}

\displaystyle=\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])},

where we have relied on the fact that

z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}})\to z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}\quad\text{in}\quad L^{\infty}_{t}([-\tau,\tau])\quad\text{as}\quad n\to\infty.

Thus, using (
6.74
), one has

\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}};z_{n})(\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)})(-\tau+s_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0.

(6.76)

Now, for
k\geqslant 1
, we write

\displaystyle(\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)})(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}})

\displaystyle=P_{c}T_{y_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}}e^{i\tau\Delta}\varphi^{(s_{0},k)}-\mathcal{L}(-\tau,0;z_{n})P_{c}T_{y_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}}\varphi^{(s_{0},k)}

\displaystyle=P_{c}T_{y_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}}(e^{i\tau\Delta}-(T_{y_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}})^{-1}\mathcal{L}(-\tau,0;z_{n})P_{c}T_{y_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}})\varphi^{(s_{0},k)}.

Thus, by Lemma
5.2
,

\lim_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert(\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}-\lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)})(\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{H^{1}}=0

(6.77)

Combining (
6.58
) at level
s_{0}
, (
6.70
), (
6.76
), and (
6.77
), we obtain

\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\varlimsup_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}};z_{n})(\xi_{n}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J})(-\tau+s_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\infty))}=0.

(6.78)

We now define the error

\displaystyle\mathcal{E}_{s_{0},n}(t)

\displaystyle{}:=((i{\partial}_{t}+H-B[z_{n}])\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{J}-\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{J}))(t+s_{n}^{s_{0}})

\displaystyle{}=\left(-(B[z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}})]-B[z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}])\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}+(\tilde{N}(z_{\infty}^{s_{0}},\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}})-\tilde{N}(z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}),\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}))\right)(t)

\displaystyle{}\quad-\bigl(\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{J})-\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)})-\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Gamma_{n}^{J})\bigr)(t+s_{n}^{s_{0}}).

We will prove below that

\sup_{\tau>0}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J})-\!\!\!\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\!\!\!\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)})-\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Gamma_{n}^{J})\right\rVert_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0.

(6.79)

By the local uniform convergence
z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}})\to z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}
, we also obtain

\displaystyle\|(B

\displaystyle[z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}})]-B[z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}])\xi_{\infty}^{0}\|_{L^{1}H^{1}([-\tau,\tau])}

\displaystyle+\left\lVert\tilde{N}(z_{\infty}^{s_{0}},\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}})-\tilde{N}(z_{n}(\cdot+s_{n}^{s_{0}}),\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([-\tau,\tau])}\to 0

as
n\to\infty
, for each
\tau>0
. Thus, by stability (Lemma
4.12
), we have

\displaystyle\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\xi_{n}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0,

(6.80)

\displaystyle\lim_{\tau\to\infty}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\xi_{n}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J}\right\rVert_{[z_{n};\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}};\infty]}=0.

(6.81)

Using this together with (
6.76
), we then obtain

\sup_{\tau>0}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}};z_{n})(\xi_{n}-\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J})(\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}})\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\infty])}=0,

which is (
6.57
) at level
s_{0}
, as desired.

It remains to prove (
6.75
) and (
6.79
).

Proof of (
6.75
).

Let
\varepsilon>0
to be determined below, and choose
\tau_{*}>0
such that

\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}((-\infty,-\tau_{*}])}\leqslant\varepsilon.

Using (
6.74
), we may also find
\tau_{**}\geqslant\tau_{*}
such that

\sup_{\tau>\tau_{**}}\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}\leqslant\varepsilon.

Thus, for
\tau\geqslant\tau_{**}
we have

\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,-\tau_{*}])}\leqslant 2\varepsilon.

By Lemma
4.11
, this implies

\left\lVert{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,-\tau_{*}])}\leqslant 4\varepsilon.

As this holds for arbitrary
\tau>\tau_{**}
, we deduce

\left\lVert{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}((-\infty,-\tau_{*}])}\leqslant 4\varepsilon,

which implies that
{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}
scatters backward in time. That is, there exists
\varphi\in H^{1}
such that

\lim_{t\to-\infty}\left\lVert{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(t)-\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi\right\rVert_{H^{1}}=0.

It remains to show that
\varphi=\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}
. To this end, we apply Strichartz to obtain

\displaystyle\bigl\|\mathcal{L}

\displaystyle(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})(\varphi-\varphi^{(s_{0},0)})\bigr\|_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}(\mathbb{R})}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}

\displaystyle\lesssim\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(-\tau,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi-{\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)\right\rVert_{H^{1}}

\displaystyle\quad+\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,-\tau;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}}){\xi}_{\infty}^{s_{0}}(-\tau)-\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([-\tau,\tau])}.

Sending
\tau\to\infty
, we obtain

\left\lVert\mathcal{L}(\cdot,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{0}})(\varphi-\varphi^{(s_{0},0)})\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}(\mathbb{R})}=0,

so that
\varphi=\varphi^{(s_{0},0)}
, as desired. ∎

Proof of (
6.79
).

Fix
\tau>0
and recall that

\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J}-\sum_{s=s_{0}+1}^{s_{\max}}\sum_{k=0}^{K(s,J)}\lambda_{n}^{(s,k)}-\Gamma_{n}^{J}=\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}.

We begin by estimating

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}

\displaystyle\bigl\|\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{J})-\tilde{N}\bigl(z_{n},\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\bigr)\bigr\|_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}

\displaystyle\lesssim\bigl(\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\bigl\|\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\bigr\|_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}\bigr)^{2}

\displaystyle\quad\quad\quad\times\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\bigl\|\sum_{s=s_{0}+1}^{s_{\max}}\sum_{k=0}^{K(s,J)}\lambda_{n}^{(s,k)}+\Gamma_{n}^{J}\bigr\|_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}.

Now, by Proposition
6.14
and Lemma
6.22
, we have

\displaystyle\sup_{J\geqslant J^{\dagger}+1}

\displaystyle\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}

\displaystyle\leqslant\left\lVert\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}(\mathbb{R})}+\sup_{K\geqslant 1}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\sum_{k=0}^{K}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}<\infty,

where we have used the assumption
\mathbb{M}(u^{s_{0}}_{\infty})<M_{*}
. Note that by (
6.51
), the supremum of the right-hand side with respect to
\tau>0
is bounded. On the other hand, we see from Lemma
6.3
that

\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\sum_{s=s_{0}+1}^{s_{\max}}\sum_{k=0}^{K(s,J)}\lambda_{n}^{(s,k)}+\Gamma_{n}^{J}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\Gamma_{n}^{J}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}.

Thus, recalling (
6.11
), we have

\sup_{\tau>0}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J})-\tilde{N}(z_{n},\tilde{\xi}_{n}^{s_{0},J}-\Gamma_{n}^{J})\right\rVert_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0.

Similarly,

\sup_{\tau>0}\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\left\lVert\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Gamma_{n}^{J})\right\rVert_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0.

Finally, we use (
6.42
), (
6.43
), and
the fact that
\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)}(\cdot-s_{n}^{s_{0}})=\xi_{\infty}^{s_{0}}\in L^{8}L^{4}(\mathbb{R})
satisfies

\lim_{R\to\infty}\sup_{n}\left\lVert{\bf 1}_{\{|x|\geqslant R\}}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},0)}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}(\mathbb{R})}=0

to see that for any
\tau>0
, we have

\lim_{J\to\infty}\varlimsup_{n\to\infty}\bigl\|\tilde{N}\bigl(z_{n},\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)}\bigr)-\sum_{k=0}^{K(s_{0},J)}\tilde{N}(z_{n},\Lambda_{n}^{(s_{0},k)})\bigr\|_{L^{\frac{8}{3}}L^{\frac{4}{3}}([\hat{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}},\tilde{\tau}_{n}^{s_{0}}])}=0.

∎

(iv) Derivation of (
6.53
).
Finally, we use (
6.57
) at level
s_{\max}
to derive (
6.53
) and complete the proof of Proposition
6.8
. The argument is the same as part (ii), in which we used (
6.57
) at level
s_{0}
to deduce (
6.58
) at level
s_{0}+1
. In particular, arguing as we did to obtain (
6.65
), we find that

\|\mathcal{L}(\cdot,\tau_{n}^{s_{\max}};z_{n})(\xi_{n}-\Gamma_{n}^{J})(\tau_{n}^{s_{\max}})\|_{L^{8}L^{4}([\tau_{n}^{s_{\max}},\infty))}\to 0

as
n,J,\tau\to\infty
, which is (
6.53
) (Essentially,
\hat{\xi}_{n}^{J,s_{\max}+1}
just reduces to
\Gamma_{n}^{J}
.) This completes the proof of Proposition
6.8
.∎

With Proposition
6.8
in hand, we are finally in a position to complete the proof of Proposition
3.7
.

Completion of the proof of Proposition
3.7
.

Let
s_{+}\in[0,s_{\max}]
be the number defined in Proposition
6.8
.

If
s_{+}=0
, then the weak
H^{1}
-convergence
u_{n}(0)\rightharpoonup u_{\infty}^{0}(0)
and the norm convergence

\lim_{n\to\infty}\mathbb{M}(u_{n}(0))=\mathrm{M}(u_{\infty}^{0}(0))

imply that the convergence
u_{n}(0)\to u_{\infty}^{0}(0)
is strong in
H^{1}
. This implies the desired result with
u_{0,\infty}=u_{\infty}^{0}(0)
. Indeed, if
u_{\infty}^{0}
scatters to
\mathscr{S}_{0}
forward in time, that is,
\left\lVert\xi_{\infty}^{0}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}[0,\infty)}<\infty,
then the stability theory implies

\sup_{n}\left\lVert\xi_{n}\right\rVert_{L^{8}L^{4}([0,\infty))}<\infty,

which is a contradiction. Thus
u_{\infty}^{0}
does not scatter to
\mathscr{S}_{0}
forward in time, and by the same argument, we obtain the failure of scattering to
\mathscr{S}_{0}
backward in time.

Suppose instead that
s_{+}\neq 0
. Without loss of generality, we may suppose that
s_{+}
is minimal in the sense that

(\mathbb{M}(u_{\infty}^{s}),\mathbb{E}_{V}(u_{\infty}^{s}))\neq(M_{*},E_{*})\quad\text{for}\quad s\in[0,s_{+}-1].

In this case, the induction argument in the proof of Proposition
6.8
works up to
s_{+}
; in particular, we have (
6.75
) for
s_{0}=s_{+}
. Then, since
\mathbb{E}_{V}(u_{\infty}^{s_{+}})=E_{*}
, we see that

\lim_{t\to-\infty}\mathbb{H}_{0}(\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{+}})\varphi^{(s_{+},0)})=\lim_{t\to-\infty}\mathbb{H}_{V}(\xi_{\infty}^{s_{+}}(t))\geqslant\mathbb{E}_{V}(u_{\infty}^{s_{+}})-C\mu=E_{*}-C\mu.

Since
s_{-}\leqslant-1
, the same argument shows

\lim_{t\to-\infty}\mathbb{H}_{0}(\mathcal{L}(t,0;z_{\infty}^{s_{+}})\varphi^{(s_{+},0)})\geqslant E_{*}-C\mu.

Combining these bounds with the energy decomposition in the linear profile decomposition, we conclude that

E_{*}\geqslant 2(1+o(\mu))(E_{*}-C\mu).

This implies
E_{*}\lesssim\mu
, which is a contradiction.∎
